Schedule PMN-1 
 
Qualifications of 
Paul M. Normand 


--- Page 3 ---
 
 
 
 
Q. 
Mr. Normand, what is your present position? 
1 
A. 
I am a principal in the consulting firm of Management Applications Consulting, Inc. 
2 
(MAC).  This Company provides consulting services to the utility industry in such fields 
3 
as loss studies, econometric studies, cost analyses, rate design, expert testimony, and 
4 
regulatory assistance.  The Company is located in Reading, Pennsylvania. 
5 
Q. 
What is your educational background? 
6 
A. 
I graduated from Northeastern University in 1975, with a Bachelor of Science Degree and 
7 
a Master of Science Degree in Electrical Engineering-Power System Analysis.  I have 
8 
attended various conferences and meetings concerning engineering and cost analysis. 
9 
Q. 
What is your professional background? 
10 
A. 
I was employed by the Massachusetts Electric Company in the Distribution Engineering 
11 
Department while attending Northeastern University.  My principal areas of assignment 
12 
included new service, voltage conversions, and system planning.  Upon graduation from 
13 
Northeastern University, I joined Westinghouse Electric Corporation Nuclear Division in 
14 
Pittsburgh, Pennsylvania.  In that position, I assisted in the procurement and economic 
15 
analysis of electrical/electronic control equipment for the nuclear reactor system.  
16 
 
 In 1976, I joined Gilbert Associates as an Engineer providing consulting services in the 
17 
rate and regulatory area to utility companies.  I was promoted to Senior Engineer in 1977, 
18 
Manager of the Austin office 1980, and Director of Rate Regulatory Service in 1981. 
19 
  
 
In June, 1983, I left Gilbert to form a separate consulting firm and I am now a 
20 
principal and President of Management Applications Consulting, Inc.  My principal areas 
21 
of concentration have been in loss studies, economic analyses, and pricing. 
22 
Q. 
Have you testified in support of any cost studies that you participated in or 
23 
performed? 
24 


--- Page 4 ---
 
 
 
 
A. 
Yes, I have testified about such studies before the following regulatory agencies:  the 
1 
Maine Public Utility Commission, the Public Utility Commission of Texas, Illinois 
2 
Commerce Commission, New Hampshire Public Utilities Commission, New Jersey 
3 
Board of Public Utilities, New York Public Service Commission, Pennsylvania Public 
4 
Utility Commission, the Massachusetts Department of Public Utilities, the Kentucky 
5 
Public Service Commission, the Arkansas Public Service Commission, the Public Service 
6 
Commission of Louisiana, the Public Utilities Commission of Ohio, the Public Service 
7 
Commission of Missouri, the Delaware Public Service Commission, the Maryland Public 
8 
Service Commission, the Indiana Utility Regulatory Commission, the North Carolina 
9 
Utilities Commission and the Federal Energy Regulatory Commission. 
10 
Q.  
Could you please briefly discuss your technical experience? 
11 
A. 
I have performed numerous accounting and marginal cost of service studies, time 
12 
differentiated bundled and fully unbundled cost studies for both electric and gas utilities 
13 
since 1980.  I have also used such studies in the design and presentation of detailed rate 
14 
proposals before regulatory agencies. 
15 
 
 My additional experience has been in the area of unaccounted for loss evaluations for 
16 
electric and gas utilities for over thirty years.  These studies include a detailed review of 
17 
each system and the calculation of appropriate recovery factors. 
18 
 
19
